# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 027 AI CHOICE SCORING
# ============================================================================
# Overview:
# - Scores every valid domain, Grand Doctrine, track, and registered
#   subdoctrine using live force, production, war, geography, theater,
#   strategy, continuity, completion, and owner-system signals.
# - Uses bounded random selection from positive scored candidates, while
#   invalid or unsupported adapter rows remain at zero weight.
# - The chooser is called again after every successful choice, so adoption
#   immediately changes the next mastery pool and score pass.
# - Strategy factors use the native observable AI force/template, doctrine,
#   naval, air, and CBRN readiness signals exposed by the current ruleset.
# ============================================================================

doctrine_research_ai_choose_scored_domain = {
	doctrine_research_ai_score_domains = yes
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.invalid }
	random_list = {
		seed = random
		doctrine_research_ai_score_army = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army } }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy } }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_air = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air } }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_special_forces = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces } }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_chaos_warfare = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare } }
	}
}

doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
	if = { limit = { num_divisions > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_military_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
	if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
	if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
	if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_has_selected_domain_strategy_signal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.strategy } }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_selected_owner_system_ready = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.owner_readiness } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_mobile_warfare compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = light_armor has_template_containing_unit = medium_armor has_template_containing_unit = heavy_armor has_template_containing_unit = motorized } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.superior_firepower compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = artillery has_template_containing_unit = anti_tank has_template_containing_unit = anti_air } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.grand_battleplan compare = equals } OR = { has_defensive_war = yes doctrine_research_controls_state_with_forest = yes doctrine_research_controls_state_with_mountain = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.mass_assault compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = infantry has_template_containing_unit = militia has_template_containing_unit = irregular_infantry } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_fleet_in_being compare = equals } OR = { has_navy_size = { unit = battleship size > 0 } has_navy_size = { unit = carrier size > 0 } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_convoy_raiding compare = equals } OR = { has_navy_size = { unit = submarine size > 0 } has_offensive_war = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_base_strike compare = equals } OR = { has_navy_size = { unit = carrier size > 0 } has_navy_size = { unit = battleship size > 0 } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_strategic_destruction compare = equals } has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = strategic_bomber } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_battlefield_support compare = equals } OR = { has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = cas } has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = tactical_bomber } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_operational_integrity compare = equals } OR = { has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = fighter } has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = naval_bomber } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quantity compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = mountaineers has_template_containing_unit = marine has_template_containing_unit = paratrooper has_template_containing_unit = ranger_battalion } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quality compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = mountaineers has_template_containing_unit = marine has_template_containing_unit = paratrooper has_template_containing_unit = ranger_battalion } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.chaos_warfare compare = equals } cbrn_chaos_warfare_ai_has_viable_program = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
}

doctrine_research_ai_choose_scored_grand = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.invalid }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_0 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_mobile_warfare }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_0 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_1 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.superior_firepower }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_1 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_2 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.grand_battleplan }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_2 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_3 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.mass_assault }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_3 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_4 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_fleet_in_being }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_4 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_5 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_convoy_raiding }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_5 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_6 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_base_strike }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_6 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_7 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_strategic_destruction }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_7 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_8 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_battlefield_support }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_8 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_9 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_operational_integrity }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_9 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_10 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quantity }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_10 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_11 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quality }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_11 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_12 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.chaos_warfare }
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_valid = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_grand = yes
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_12 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	random_list = {
		seed = random
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_0 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_mobile_warfare } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_1 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.superior_firepower } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_2 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.grand_battleplan } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_3 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.mass_assault } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_4 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_fleet_in_being } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_5 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_convoy_raiding } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_6 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_base_strike } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_7 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_strategic_destruction } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_8 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_battlefield_support } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_9 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_operational_integrity } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_10 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quantity } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_11 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quality } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
		doctrine_research_ai_grand_weight_12 = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.chaos_warfare } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found = 1 } }
	}
}

doctrine_research_ai_score_selected_track = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
	if = { limit = { num_divisions > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_military_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_naval_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
	if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
	if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
	if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_has_selected_domain_strategy_signal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.strategy } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain value = doctrine_research_selected_domain compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_last_transaction_track value = doctrine_research_selected_track compare = equals } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.continuity } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = infantry has_template_containing_unit = motorized has_template_containing_unit = militia has_template_containing_unit = irregular_infantry } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.combat_support compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = artillery has_template_containing_unit = anti_tank has_template_containing_unit = anti_air } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.armor compare = equals } OR = { has_template_containing_unit = light_armor has_template_containing_unit = medium_armor has_template_containing_unit = heavy_armor has_template_containing_unit = mechanized } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.operations compare = equals } OR = { has_offensive_war = yes has_defensive_war = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.submarines compare = equals } has_navy_size = { unit = submarine size > 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.screens compare = equals } has_navy_size = { unit = destroyer size > 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.carriers compare = equals } has_navy_size = { unit = carrier size > 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.capital_ships compare = equals } has_navy_size = { unit = battleship size > 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.fighter_aircraft compare = equals } has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = fighter } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.strike_aircraft compare = equals } OR = { has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = cas } has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = naval_bomber } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.medium_aircraft compare = equals } has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = tactical_bomber } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.heavy_aircraft compare = equals } has_deployed_air_force_size = { size > 0 type = strategic_bomber } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }

doctrine_research_ai_choose_scored_subdoctrine = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_subdoctrine_found = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_subdoc_weight_0 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.mobile_infantry }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } OR = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine_is_active_incomplete = yes doctrine_research_selected_empty_subdoctrine_is_valid = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_template_containing_unit = infantry has_template_containing_unit = motorized has_template_containing_unit = militia has_template_containing_unit = irregular_infantry } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_military_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
		if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.continuity } }
		if = { limit = { has_mastery_level = { amount = 1 sub_doctrine = mobile_infantry } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.continuity } }
		if = { limit = { has_mastery_level = { amount = 4 sub_doctrine = mobile_infantry } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.completion } }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_subdoc_weight_0 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_subdoc_weight_1 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.defensive_postures }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } OR = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine_is_active_incomplete = yes doctrine_research_selected_empty_subdoctrine_is_valid = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_template_containing_unit = infantry has_template_containing_unit = motorized has_template_containing_unit = militia has_template_containing_unit = irregular_infantry } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_military_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
		if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.continuity } }
		if = { limit = { has_mastery_level = { amount = 1 sub_doctrine = defensive_postures } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.continuity } }
		if = { limit = { has_mastery_level = { amount = 4 sub_doctrine = defensive_postures } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.completion } }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_subdoc_weight_1 = doctrine_research_ai_candidate_score }

	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_subdoc_weight_2 = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.large_unit_tactics }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } OR = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine_is_active_incomplete = yes doctrine_research_selected_empty_subdoctrine_is_valid = yes } }
